Stable pass (`?stable=1`) left OFF (owner has not OKed shimmer). Tests: gfxPresets, pointShadowOpt, gfxBoot, settings, settingsMount PASS.
Perf: no point-shadow GPU p95 number exists in docs/test-reports (only the 0.15 ms iGPU target for flicker, open); 8 ms p95 for high/ultra is UNMEASURED. ASK ARCHITECT: need a WebGpuTimer p95 run before keeping it ON. Verify: `node tools/verify-pointshadows.mjs 9500` (screenshots, with vs without).

- AUD-44 fallout + QUALITY-BOOT-01 (kestrel-1): (a) verify-pointshadows "off" passes ?pointshadows=0; main.js uses pointShadowOpt.presetPointShadowsOn (capture/bench/gpucompare ignore the preset knob unless ?quality=). (b) Boot root causes: pendingBootGrid was applied in update() AFTER the title-menu early return (stayed at CPU grid behind the menu); fixed by moving it to the top of update(). Owner "Ultra smaller than High": integer cell px (1717x790: 400x150 -> 1600x750, 480x180 -> 1440x720) + CSS never upscaled; fitCssSize gets maxUp (WebGPU live window = 2, smooth sampling) so the picture fills the window height at every preset.
  Verify: `node tools/verify-quality-boot.mjs 95xx` (7 variants, asserts 480x180/400x150 + ultra height >= high); `node tools/verify-pointshadows.mjs 95xx`. gpucompare result: see report.

TELEPORT-BUG 2026-10-11 (kestrel-4, teleport-bug) NOT REPRODUCED in Node: world_m1 + installGeo + real vitals sim (void rule, fall damage, respawn), player from the tower meadow (1470,1029) walking 16 headings x 200 s on the mesh physics with band streaming: no pose jump > 5 m, no hp change, never dead (W/SW end at the ford x 1042 z -87, others stop at the capsule-union walk bound; identical with geo off). Code audit: nothing outside vitals.respawn (needs death), vitals void rule (lastSafe, no heal), travel.teleport (M-map only), placeLoaded (load only; no-op for the default union bounds) writes the player pose; the bounds clamp only projects. Added a route-walk leg "probe geo walk" (geo installed + vitals) so this stays covered. One browser check (headless pane, fps 0) could not walk. Remaining hypotheses for the owner: (1) a death + the DEATH-FLOW-01 auto-respawn (fade -> virtual [E]) read as a teleport with full HP (what hurt him? beast/fall damage - ask for the HP before); (2) an autosaved slot / `R` restart; (3) the async browser band swap (east>west, Node swaps instantly): a missing floor for a few frames -> fall -> void rule snaps to lastSafe. Next step: owner repro with ?debug=1 and note the pose text before/after.

- TELEPORT-BUG (waystone/relay walk, kestrel-4, NOT reproduced): new `tools/verify-waystone-walk.mjs <port> [fromX] [toX] [y] [woken]` loads `?debug=1&dev=1&save=1`, stops the rAF loop, steps `loop.update(1/60)` by hand with real W-key physics walking west, fails on a pose jump > 3 m or death. Runs: tower -> stone (stone dormant and woken: walk-in touch fires, anchor = pose at touch, no teleport), tower -> Bend relay (1263,1027), road to x=985 (stops at 1150,1047 on an obstacle): no jump, no teleport, hp 30 (one boar2 hit 5 hp at x~1452 in the pose-stepped run). Only code that moves the player: vitals.respawn (death), vitals void rule (z < lastSafe.z-30 -> lastSafe), travel.teleport (map only), placeLoaded (load only). Best remaining hypotheses: (1) death by boars near the stone then auto death-flow respawn at the tower (full HP, beasts reset) - owner's save state differs; (2) void rule with a stale lastSafe=tower pose if grounded/fallDistance never clears on slopes. Needs the owner's console (`__debug.vitals.dead`, `world.state['save.x']`) or save JSON. No engine/game code changed.

## OB-101 reach (branch ob101-reach, 2026-10-12)
- Cause: global carpet maxH = 0.9 * 1.25 * 1.8 = ~2.0 m (straw + tuft mul), so a standing eye (1.7 m) is inside the layer for the whole ray: segment = eye -> ground hit, 60-170 lattice steps; NMAX 24 dropped every ray beyond ~2.4 m.
- Fix: NMAX 24 -> 128 and a ray needing more is TRUNCATED (walks the first 128 cells, nearest first) instead of dropped; JS (carpet.js) and WGSL (carpet.wgsl.js) twins identical (`nSteps = min(steps, NMAX)`). No other shader/layout change. zTop uses ground at tHit (exact on flat, conservative-ish on slopes; left as is).
- Cost (240x90 view, eye 1.7 m flat meadow, pitch 0.2/0.38 rad): hits 45 -> 7610 and 424 -> 8516 rays; mean walked steps 74/66, mean blade tests 222/197 per ground ray (max 169 steps; 3.3-3.6k rays/frame truncated). Before: most rays returned at once (<= 24 steps, <= ~80 tests). So carpet pass cost on grass cells grows roughly 10-20x (bounded by 128 steps x ~3.4 tests); not measured on a GPU. Cheap follow-up if needed: per-variant height reject before the hash chain in bladeHit, or per-class maxH.

- SWIM-04c parity fix (gpucompare underwaterLake rows, cmpCells FAIL; evidence rtx4060 log): root cause = GPU water composite runs AFTER shade (and its under tint) and overwrote every water-covered cell (the lake surface seen from below = the whole top of pitch 0, every cell at pitch 35) with untinted composite colours; JS ran composite BEFORE the under pass so under (ceiling branch) won. Fix, one order in both twins: a submerged eye (fb.under.on) skips the surface composite. JS: compositor.js `submerged` gates `meshWater` (waterMask null); GPU: WaterCompositeU `ripplePad0` renamed `underOn` (same word, layout unchanged), passWater writes fb.under.on, waterComposite.wgsl returns the shade cell early. Edge pass still sees the water texture on GPU (JS mask null): only matters with detailPass. Node: underwater.test +3 source-order checks; filter underwater,waterComposite,passWater,shade.wgsl,compositor PASS, check-deps ok.
  Main-session gate: `node tools/capture-browser.mjs --mode gpucompare --backend webgpu --timeout-ms 600000 --port {port} --baseline docs/test-reports/gpucompare-baseline-webgpu-rtx4060.json`; expected: both underwaterLake rows PASS (or only lattice-edge floor(P/0.7) tie counts <1 %), all other rows unchanged (water poses have fb.under null so submerged=false). -> arch-review + gpucompare
- SWIM-04c follow-up (pitch-0 row left: 576 cells, k9 181, same glyph, colour scaled darker/brighter per cell, bands 4-6 unchanged by the composite fix): hypothesis = pass ORDER at mesh edges: GPU shade -> under -> edge (edge scales the tinted colour), JS ran edgePass BEFORE sky/under (edge scaled untinted, then tint blend). Not provable in Node (no render). Fix: compositor.js, when `submerged`, runs fillSky + underwaterPass before edgePass and skips the later copies (`underDone`); underwater.test checks the order. Dry frames untouched. Re-run the same gpucompare command; expected pitch-0 row PASS. If k9 cells still differ it is NOT the order: next suspects = pre-tint mesh fg or depth L for mesh cells (compare fg before under via ?gpucompare shade-only dump) -> ASK ARCHITECT. -> arch-review + gpucompare
- SWIM-04c v3 (v2 left ~0.85x darkening on k9 cells at y=0 / ceiling rows, CPU darker): cause = edge pass suppression mismatch. GPU edge pass still got waterOn=1 + the WATER texture when submerged (edge skips opaque-water cells), JS had waterMask=null so edgePass darkened/outlined those mesh cells seen through the surface. Fix: passShade.js edge uniform `waterOn = wOn && !(U && U.on)` so submerged = no water edge suppression in both twins. underwater.test +1 source check; filter underwater,passShade,shade.wgsl,passWater PASS. Same gpucompare command, expect both rows PASS. Not covered: passStable waterOn (stable pass off in gpucompare). -> arch-review + gpucompare

- LOD-RESET-01 (9ceae836): ARCH OK. WgCellPipeline.resetLodHistory also resets the shadow pass cull (WgShadowPass.resetLodHistory -> cull.resetLodHistory). onCut resets the GPU twin whenever wgActive, even with occlusion/stable off. The passCull test checks that the shadow band lodPrev is zeroed.
- OB-184b (019b932b): ARCH OK. validateDetailConfig rejects a non-object cluster, a cluster on a gpu layer, negative or NaN keys, and treeR outside (0, 8]. _onTrunk now uses the shared _treeGridOf (same 8 m grid and key). Tests cover the bad cases.
- SAPLINGS (08707661): ARCH CHANGES. (1) The 37.4.1 "Bounds" rule is not implemented: the detail group radius (instances.js groupRadius -> g._R) ignores `scale`, but validateDetailConfig allows max <= 2, so species with s > 1 would be culled too early. Either multiply the group R by the largest species scale for detail groups, or cap the validator at max <= 1 and update 37.4.1. Either way, add one test. Everything else is OK. Placements without `scale` stay byte-identical (slot 6 is drawn only when a species has `scale`; the test compares x/y/z/yaw/species/r2/tileStart). The GPU path reads the scaled iRow0..2 from the instance row (raster.wgsl.js instanced branch) and normalizes the normal, as does rasterJS per pixel, so no WGSL change is needed. Sway height scales with the instance, which is fine. Colliders scale r/hx/hy/h (the 0.5 m skirt below the base stays fixed, which is fine). writeScaledInstance(s=1) matches writeUnitInstance word for word. There is no new per-frame allocation: scatter is load-time, and the feed writes rows in place.
- SAPLINGS arch change done -> re-review (g.radiusScale = max per-instance detail scale, set in scatterFeed.js bindDetailInstances, applied to g._R in instances.js x3; test in scatterFeed.test.js).

- OB-166 (bca518ed): ARCH OK. Missing species get groupOf 255, but they can never be indexed: whenever something is missing, `keep` is forced non-null and their placements stay 0. The scale loop, the master write and feedDetail all check keep first. Species to group mapping is per-species, so there is no index shift. The warn fires once per bind (load time). There is precedent for engine tests importing design/, so this is not required now.

Crocs are ordinary beast slots (waterBrain pool slot == beast slot), so they count toward MAX_BEASTS = 320 (farCamps.test asserts that the full world fits). Their homes are deep (mask 1, plus/minus 1 m) inside WATER_NAV_AREA, so initSwimmer never falls back to a land croc. Land movement is limited by the existing waterBrain canStand rule (croc grid + nearWaterM). Generation is seeded (mulberry32 per rule key), and spawn and planWorldLife share the same probe. No step-path change; the beastView lookup does not allocate. The merge resolution is correct: water rules skip regionAt and use wcentres; road rng uses landCount. Note (not blocking): nextGroup[region] is shared, so croc groups shift the `group` index of later reedmire/heartland land groups whenever a water probe is present. Ids and positions do not change. If director pack keys must match between probe and no-probe callers, give crocs their own counter.

- BEAST-POOL-b ARCH CHANGES (PC-B architect, 2026-10-15, 3acf2319): (1) poolUpdate step 1 drops dead records (DYING/CORPSE/SINK) for the off-tile reason; 37.16.5 says they keep their slot. Since the binder runs before emitPendingDied, a kill followed by a tile swap / tick%30 step loses `beast:died` (loot, quest counts), and the corpse never reaches GONE because the candidate scan skips deadSt. Fix: keep `deadSt(state) || pendingDied || despawnReq` records bound regardless of reason. Test: kill + off-tile update in the same step -> one died event, corpse reaches GONE. (2) `sim.save/load` (dev snapshot) restores the pool-indexed `path` and `steer.active` but not the binding. Loading into a sim with a different binding gives mismatched paths and active-but-unbound ghost agents. Fix: snapshot pool `lv/rec/n/free/freeN/lastX/lastY` + `dormTile/dormant` (in-memory only; the persistent save stays slot-free). Test: 300-record round trip into a fresh sim, hash equal. Accepted as is: the nav-tile reason overriding protection (same as WM-04; it cannot path home off-tile, the snap is a known pop); a full protected pool -> `full++` (no eviction of fighters is correct); bind order deterministic; the reserve gap (<= 1 update); O(count) only inside the 30-step binder; simShape passes.

- SIM-SPIKE-01-FIX (kestrel-3, merged bh2e1 for HUNT-ALLOC-01): status arch-review. Finding: the 4-20 ms "beast max" spikes are OS/JIT noise, not work bursts. Phase timers put them in the post loop (`supportAtOut`, 0.02 ms call), repeating the same call at the same spot right after a 5 ms spike took 0.01 ms; A* never exceeded 2 ms; GC scavenges are 0.3 ms. So no sim code changed and there is nothing to slice (beastGolden 83261736 unchanged, state hash identical). Fix = measurement: `tools/bench-life.mjs --repeat N` takes the per-step minimum over N identical deterministic runs (real bursts stay, noise drops); `--trace` lists steps over 4 ms with the A* delta. 24 sim-h, `--strict --repeat 3 --scenarios 1 --no-goblin`: beast p50 0.281 / p99.9 0.549 / max 0.790 ms (cap 4), life max 0.177, fauna 0.042, villagers 0.039, A* max 1/step; exit 0. ### ARCH batch round 4b 2026-10-15 (PC-B, opus)
- **bh2e1 (k2) HUNT-ALLOC-01 + BH2-E1: ARCH CHANGES** - (1) `createLogicWire` subscribes `o.sim.events.on('beasts:reset', ...)` but `sim.events` is the shared `engine.events` and the wire is rebuilt on every `world:loaded` (main.js ~1638): old wires never unsubscribe, so each world load leaks a handler that re-parks groups on a stale sim (and pins the old sim/world). Keep the `off` returned by `events.on` (as telegraphWire.js does) and call it from the wire's teardown (logicUnreg path); add a logicWire.test case "two wires, first disposed, reset fires once". HUNT-ALLOC-01 part OK (same truth table as `_terrainSupports`; note: keepMask structures still call the double-arg helper, fine while P5 passes).
- **bhr1 (k2) BH-R1: ARCH OK** - notes: the in-flight `_bandJob` (pending switch) is not dropped, so a placement during a swap shows on the bake after; acceptable (runtime placement is rare). Remove the no-op `|| true` assertion in bandPlaceStructure.test.
- **pcafix2 (k2) BH2-R1 + BEAST-ALLOC-FIX2: ARCH CHANGES** - (1) `publishBand` refills look only when `!b.look`: a second `setGroundLook(cfg2)` leaves cached bands with cfg1 look on cache hit. Tag the bundle with the baker (`b.lookOf = this.groundLook`) and refill when `b.lookOf !== this.groundLook`; extend lookBake.test 2b. (2) BEAST-ALLOC-FIX2 probe-size edits in beastSim.core/hunt tests conflict with blendalloc (k1 37f6c96a) on the same lines: on merge keep the blendalloc chunk/settle form (n 1500 + chunk) and drop the n bumps unless they still fail. passRaster guard OK.
- **pcafix1 (k1) GI-PROBE-03-FIX / 06-FIX / FOOTIK-WIRE-FIX: ARCH OK** - `_gridFill` load is guarded by the synchronous PENDING state (one fetch per zone); GI_CACHE 8192 fine; support-ground adapter is 0-alloc, stepUp 1.0 from refZ+0.6 is sane.
- **blendalloc (k1) allocProbe chunk/settle + uses + flowField.begin: ARCH OK** - verified: `settle` only adds warm-up windows before the unchanged min-of-`windows` measurement, so steady per-call garbage is in every measured window and still fails; `chunk` keeps the same call count per window and can only add run() overhead, never remove call-site garbage (the leak test with chunk+settle proves it). Rule for users (already in the JSDoc): no chunk on run()s with per-call setup. flowField freshness key (L, from, to, nLinks) matches reverseLinks' cache key.
- **ob152density (k1) WIND-GUST-01b leafFall: ARCH OK** - 0 alloc, bounded scan (768/check at 5 Hz), cap ring correct, gustBand injected (rule 16 kept), calm = no spawn. Nits (no rework): after a hit the cursor stays on the same tree, so one tree sheds every 0.2 s while its gust lasts (advance `i` before break if the PO wants spread); test file lives in engine/world/ for an engine/fx module.
- **bh2g1 + diffknobs (k3) BH2-G1: ARCH CHANGES** - (1) **Bug:** beastSim.js `noticedOf`: the edit put `// BH2-G1:` before `a.hearR = ...; a.coneCos = ...;`, commenting them out - per-slot hearing/cone (bears, archetypes, calm boars) now read whatever the shared `_pa` scratch last held; Normal is NOT byte-identical for non-default slots. Restore both statements (comment at line end). Add a test with a non-default `pNear`/`pCone` slot. (2) vitals `dmgMul` applies playerDmg to every non-player source incl. beasts, but beast hits already carry Enemy damage (`dmg`, rescaled in setDifficulty): Hard beast hits become x1.35*1.25. Epic table 4: playerDmg = falls/drowning/fire/traps only -> skip dmgMul when the source is a beast (or apply it only at the environmental damage entries). (3) main.js `dmgMul` calls `loadSettings()` (storage + JSON parse) per hit: read `beasts.difficulty.playerDmg` instead. Advisory: groupDelta consumes the shared `rng` in generateRegionalBeasts, so on Easy/Hard every later group's homes shift vs Normal; draw extra members after the base ones or from a per-group sub-rng if save/home stability matters. Elite = +2 levels (spec says x1.5 HP) and Easy flank = stacked 8 m ring (spec: straight chase) are PO calls. Zero alloc + determinism otherwise OK (diffMults allocates only on setDifficulty; elite roll seeded by slot).
- **pcafix3 (k3) SIM-SPIKE-01-FIX: ARCH OK** - tools-only. Note: element-wise min-of-N is valid only if repeats are identical; assert the per-run state hash matches across repeats (fail loudly otherwise), and remember min also hides GC pauses that move between runs (GC is covered by the alloc tests, not this gate).
- WM-ARCH-FIX: verified already done in b248a5ba/36c521eb/e6683327 (slice counter, no now closure, NavTileFill+navLate, 12-zone walk); band/world/nav/terrain suites 104 PASS; no code change.

### ARCH answer: pitched water alloc (PC-B 5th agent, PC-A to ratify)
2026-10-15, opus, re RENDER-ALLOC-01-GATE (k3 f41b32d2) + WATER-ALLOC-01-FIX.
1. **Policy (recommend PC-A ratifies):** the JS-twin composite is a gpucompare oracle only (no player path since "WebGPU required"). Alloc gates on oracle-only paths become **warn-only**: `waterComposite.alloc.test` pitched + the RENDER-ALLOC-01 "pitched" pose log the bytes and print WARN above the limit, but do not fail. Byte-parity (gpucompare / waterFinish parity asserts) stays a hard gate. Same rule for RENDER-ALLOC-02. Player-path JS (sim, physics, entities, CPU-side GPU upload) keeps hard alloc gates.
2. **Not worth more programmer time now.** Drop the hand-inlined draft (wc_mine.js): a too-big loop that V8 will not inline just moves the boxing into waterFinish (40-78 KB). Do not raise LIMIT; mark the test WARN-only with a comment citing this answer.
3. **If PC-A wants it fixed anyway (<= 0.5 d, one step):** keep the loop small and remove the remaining double-crossing calls, the same way WATER-ALLOC-01 did:
   a) `engine/render/projection.js`: add `unprojectPitchedIO(pterms, col, row, io)` and `unprojectCellIO(terms, col, row, io)` (depth read from `io[3]`, xyz written to `io[0..2]`, return bool). `unprojW` in waterComposite.js calls them with `_w`-style scratch, no double arguments.
   b) `reflectAt`: give `waterReflect.js` a `waterReflectAtIO(io, lut, lutTop, out)` that reads eyeZ/dx/dy/dz from a Float64Array (4 double args today). `riverHit`/`reflOk`: same pattern (read `_w[2]`/`_w[0]` inside waterLook.js through an io block, not as an argument).
   c) If it still allocates, split the per-cell body into two module-level monomorphic functions `surfCell(i)` / `skyCell(i)` (int args only, state in module Float64Array scratch), each small enough to inline. Probe with `--trace-deopt --trace-turbo-inlining`; pass = pitched <= 1024 B/call on Node 22 with forced TurboFan, plus waterFinish.test <= 512 B.

## ARCH batch round 6 2026-10-15 (PC-B, opus)
1. **passLight.cam** - **integ5 kestrel-1 40197039 is the one to keep: ARCH OK.** Plain object with double-initialised fields (0.5) = V8 double-representation fields, stores overwrite in place: 0 B/frame, no boxing, no getter call in shadePass. 3facc258 (Float64Array + getters) was also correct and allocation-free, just more indirection; drop it at merge. Nit: the comment above `this.cam` still says "Float64Array, getters" - fix it in the next touch.
2. **CANOPY-SHAFT-01b-FIX e99df840 - ARCH CHANGES (the measurement point is legit, the gate needs tightening).** The lift is a shade-stage op, so 38.76 rule 8a "moves toward tint*sunI" is correctly judged on the shade output; the edge pass multiplies fg by a gain >= 0 after it, which keeps the sign but can overshoot. Not a weakening only if the final cells are still checked. Required: (1) keep a no-slack final-cell assert: for every changed channel, sign(final B - final A) == sign(shade B - shade A) (edge gain is a positive multiply, so this holds exactly; 0 tolerance) and no final change where the shade delta is 0. (2) `readShade` mandatory in gpucompare: if `readbackShade` returns null the row FAILs (no silent fallback to final cells; today `sA.fg` would throw). (3) Amend architecture.md 38.76 rule 8a in the same commit: "direction measured on the shade-stage readback; final cells: sign match + glyph/changed-cell/>=1 % asserts". PC-A re-reviews.
3. **NIGHT-HAZE-01 f5de91c4 - ARCH OK.** Engine module is data-free (spec passed in), 0 alloc per apply, day weight 1 restores captured bytes exactly, version bump only on change. Note for the record: with ENV-03 atmos on, the hour reaches the biome haze only because `atmosBase.near/far` hold the SAME arrays fogHour writes in place - add one comment line at `atmosBase = {...}` saying so (otherwise a later `.slice()` there silently breaks night haze). Test nit: drop `assert.ok(v1 > v0 || true)`.
4. **RENDER-ALLOC-01-GATE f41b32d2 - ARCH OK.** SC/FS scratch is the established WATER-ALLOC-01 pattern; direct CellBuffer write matches `setCellRGB` + `clampByte` exactly (Math.round, clamp, glyph in fg.a, bg.a 255, mask 1), guarded by `rt.constructor === CellBuffer` and size. Min-of-4 gc'd windows is a fair steady-state probe, LIMIT unchanged, 4.6 s. Follow-up: add a comment in `CellBuffer.setCellRGB` "inlined twin in detailShade.shadeSurfaces"; the real Node 24 run stays an open owner/PC-A to-do.
5. **SOFTLOCK-01 99e84ead - ARCH CHANGES.** (1) `deadSeen` is not saved, so kill wolves -> save -> reload -> accept still soft-locks if authored packs stay dead after load. Either persist the kill record (a `deadSeen` id list in the book save) or prove with a test that the pack respawns after load; add the save/load case to errands3.test. (2) `livingOfKind` reaches the book only when questPickups.sync() runs; accept through dialogue on a freshly loaded book before the first sync falls back to OB-115. Install the hook where the book is created/loaded (saveRelay), not lazily. (3) Nit: `&& s.state[i] !== STATE_EAT` is dead (13 is outside 9..12); use the existing `deadSt()` semantics instead of a range test on enum order.
6. **Warden Talk + char.kit clip alias ec5279d8 - ARCH OK** (game-only, fixes a real hard block). Follow-up (see item 7): make the loop generic - every giver `npc`/`turnIn` entity with `components.dialogue` that is not life-managed gets `addNpc`, not only `/^warden_/`; and NPC display names now live in 3 tables (dialogueCtl NPC_NAMES, errands LABELS, story.md) - move them to one content source next time either is touched.
7. **BEND-NPC-01 1e73856a + 751dab1f - ARCH CHANGES.** questSnippets accept/decline change is correct (per-quest `.later` node only when `lines.decline` exists; usedLater kept for the rest). (1) I find no `[E] Talk` registration for `joss` (not in lifeWire npcs, no `addNpc('joss')`, and errands.load skips entities with a dialogue component) - same bug class as the wardens; verify in a walk and fix by generalising the item-6 loop to all dialogue-bearing giver entities (the same gap looks true for `postkeeper`), with a test that every giver.npc/turnIn entity with a dialogue gets a Talk interactable. (2) Add `lines.accept` / `lines.decline` to the quest-def schema/validator if one lists `lines` keys.
8. **UNDER-LIGHT-CREEP-01 wiring b5697651 - ARCH CHANGES.** Mist path is fine (base returned exactly at 0). Lights are not: `applyCreepLights` scales by ratios (`A *= new/old`), so leaving a crypt does `A *= 1/s` after `A *= s` - not bit-exact, flicker drift accumulates per visit, and lights added to `lightSet` while inside get divided without having been multiplied. Required: (1) capture base ambient/flickerAmount when the scale leaves 1 (or on each hour rewrite, where `st.amb = 1` is set today) and write `base * scale` absolutely; at `out.w === 0` restore the base bytes exactly. (2) Test: enter -> fade in -> exit -> fade out, assert `lightSet.ambient` and `flickerAmount` are bitwise equal to the pre-entry values (and with an hour step during the visit).
9. **SHIELD-STAMINA-01 ac2dfe34 - ARCH CHANGES; UNDER-SKEL-VARIETY-01 6df841b8 - ARCH OK.** Shield: the stamina part is inert (needs `attachStamina`), but the vitals arrow branch is live today: a frontal arrow on a raised shield now deals 50 % instead of 0. (1) Gate the `arrowFactor` branch on stamina being attached (or get a PO/owner OK that this ships now and say so in the row). Skeleton variety: no new sim top-level keys (simShape 140 <= 145), `blockMul` runs only on damage (no per-step cost, no alloc), telegraph/spdF set at spawn. Note: `blockMul` uses `_pp` (player position of the last step) for every damage source, so a fire/beast-sourced hit is also cut when the skeleton faces the player - acceptable for now, document in the kinds.json `blockF` note.

## FLICKER-METRIC-DET (OB-208, kestrel-4 branch flickerdet, uncommitted)
Root cause of bimodal results: the grid. flickerlive is not captureLike, so grid came from preset/saved/boot race: ~half the runs measured 240x90 (wallNear 7.5-7.7) vs 480x180 (5.06). Fix: capture-browser flicker live pins `grid=480x180`; main.js waits for the live grid (setGrid) before the 60 warm-up frames.
Also: `flickerLive.hold` skips wgPipeline.frame during capture (readbacks see ONE frame); capture retries <=20x on throw/null readback (gpuFormat-null guard); settle 30->60; per-pose cols/rows/used diagnostics.
`tools/flicker-gate.mjs --repeat K` (median + min/max spread, retries a failed browser start twice and reports count). New baseline values (480x180, stable, rays 4): wallNear 5.06 wallFar 4.92 bushes 4.18 crown 4.99 grassFar 3.73 avg 4.58 - the stored flicker-baseline.json predates this and must be re-saved (--save --repeat 3).

### OB-203-REOPEN (kestrel-1, 2026-10-16) - po-review
Cause: inventoryScreen.step ends with input.consumePressed(), which also clears isDown('Mouse0') for the whole press, so the drag was released the very next step (always a click, never a drop). Node test mock hid it. Fix: screen takes `o.mouseHeld` (main.js feeds real button state from window mousedown/mouseup); fallback to isDown. Pack->empty cell with gaps now sticks; model/save never compacted (plain JSON slots).
Files: game/js/quest/inventoryScreen.js, game/js/main.js, inventoryScreen.test.js (+OB-203 cases), tools/verify-backpack-drag.mjs (PASS on 9510, ran 3x incl. 1 pre-fix FAIL repro). Suites inventory,save + check-deps OK.
- 2026-10-16 OB-209 (far silhouette) kestrel-3 -> `arch-review` (engine/render/atmos.js). What draws it: far world_geo terrain (gone with ?geo=0; not skyline cap (null), not impostors (?imp=0 same), no far_ferrum). Root cause: ENV-03 atmos fog replaced the far fog end with the biome fog colour while the sky horizon = look sky t=0 + atmos haze, so fully fogged hills ended a different (green/pale) colour than the sky -> flat cut-out.
  Fix: applyAtmosToPalette sets the far fog end (liveFogFar) = skyHorizonRGB(P, state) weighted by the biome share (1 - wNone, new state.wNone; wNone 1 byte-identical), palette-only -> JS twin + WGSL read the same uniforms (no shader change, gpucompare parity unaffected; not re-run). Night/dusk follow liveLook automatically.
  Gate: `node tools/verify-far-silhouette.mjs 9650 after` (exit 1 if far-hill vs sky-above rgb distance > 36 at 12/19/22) + tools/farSilhouette.test.mjs + atmos.test OB-209 block. Numbers: before 12:00 40.7 FAIL -> after 12:00 30.7, 19:00 25.7, 22:00 ok. Residual: hill at ~f 0.9 still a faint flat band (owner look).
  Open: navy far trees NOT reproduced at this pose (quality=high; mid trees read dark green) - tree/impostor shading untouched; non-atmos fogHour dusk far #d89870 != skyDuskHorizon (design data, not changed). before-19 still was taken without save=0 = actually noon. Stills docs/test-reports/ob209-far-{before,after}-*.png (+ atmos0/imp0/geo0 diagnostics).
